\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad\qquad\left(\varepsilon_{u}+\mathbb{E}_{\bf Y}\left[\left|\theta_{\delta,N}^{\bf Y}(x_{j}^{u})-\theta_{\delta,N}^{\bf Y}(x_{q}^{u-1})\right|\right]+\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bf Y},{\bf Z}}\left[\left\|\sigma_{\delta,N}(x_{j}^{u})-\sigma_{\delta,N}(x_{q}^{u-1})\right\|\right]\right)

(171)

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{f\in{\rm BL}}I_{u-1}\right]+M\left(\varepsilon_{u}^{\min(a,b)}+\delta^{\min(a,b)}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\delta^{d}}}\right)\mathbb{E}\left[M_{u}\right]\>.

By induction from
u=t
down to
s
on (
171
) and recalling that
I_{s}\leq MM_{s}
, we have the following control:

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{f\in{\rm BL}}I_{t}\right]\leq M\left(\mathbb{E}\left[M_{s}\right]+\left(\delta^{\min(a,b)}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\delta^{d}}}\right)\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[M_{u}\right]+\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{\min(a,b)}\mathbb{E}\left[M_{u}\right]\right)\>.

We recall (
46
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[M_{u}\right]\leq\frac{M}{N^{\frac{1}{2}}}\varepsilon_{u}^{-\frac{d}{2}}\mu\left(D^{\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{4}}\right)
(and
N^{\frac{1}{2}}=\varepsilon^{-\frac{d}{2}}
)
and moreover
\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{4}\leq\delta
so that

\mu\left(D^{\frac{\varepsilon_{s}}{4}}\right)\leq M\mu(D^{\delta})
.
Consequently

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{f\in{\rm BL}}I_{t}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{M}{\sqrt{N}}\mu\left(D^{\delta}\right)\left(\varepsilon_{s}^{-\frac{d}{2}}+\left(\delta^{\min(a,b)}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\delta^{d}}}\right)\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{-\frac{d}{2}}+\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}}\right)

(172)

\displaystyle\leq M\mu\left(D^{\delta}\right)\left(\varepsilon^{(1-\alpha)\frac{d}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}\left(\delta^{\min(a,b)}+\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\delta^{\frac{d}{2}}}\right)\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{-\frac{d}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}}\right)\>.

Note that for
\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2}=(3^{-u})^{-d/2}=(3^{d/2})^{u}
and
3^{d/2}\geq\sqrt{3}>1
so that

\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2}\leq\sum_{u=0}^{t}(3^{d/2})^{u}\leq\frac{(3^{d/2})^{t+1}}{3^{d/2}-1}\leq\frac{3^{d/2}}{3^{d/2}-1}\varepsilon_{t}^{-d/2}\leq 3\varepsilon^{-d/2}\leq 3N^{1/2}\quad\text{and}\quad N^{1/2}=\varepsilon^{-\frac{d}{2}}\>,

and together with (
172
) we can infer (
170
).

Conclusion of Step
1
and
2
:

We can now draw an intermediate conclusion in the proof of (
7.4
). Indeed, thanks to (
7
), (
160
) and (
170
),

\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{\delta,N},\nu_{\delta}\right)\right]\\
\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}(\widehat{W}_{\delta,N},W_{\delta,\delta})\right]\end{array}\right\}\leq M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{20C\delta})\\
+M\mu(D^{\delta})\left(\varepsilon^{(1-\alpha)\frac{d}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\min(a,b)}+\delta^{\min(a,b)}+\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\delta^{\frac{d}{2}}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}}\right)\>,

and thanks to Proposition
6.12
and
6.13
(with
r=\delta
) and triangular inequality, we infer the same control for
\nu
,
W_{S}
instead of
\nu_{\delta}
,
W_{\delta,\delta}
:

(173)

\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{\delta,N},\nu\right)\right]\\
\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}(\widehat{W}_{\delta,N},W_{S})\right]\end{array}\right\}\leq M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{20C\delta})\\
+M\mu(D^{\delta})\left(\varepsilon^{(1-\alpha)\frac{d}{2}}+\varepsilon^{\min(a,b)}+\delta^{\min(a,b)}+\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\delta^{\frac{d}{2}}}+\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}}\right)\>.

It remains to simplify the last sum above depending on
\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}
.

Step
3
:
we now prove that

(174)

\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{\delta,N},\nu\right)\right]\\
\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}(\widehat{W}_{\delta,N},W_{S})\right]\end{array}\right\}\leq M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{20C\delta})+M\delta^{\min(a,b)}\>\mu(D^{\delta})\quad\text{with}\quad\delta=\delta_{N}=N^{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{d+2\min(a,b)}}\>.

– Case
\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}<0
:
note that
\min(a,b)\leq 1
whence the current case covers
d>2
,
d=2
and
\min(a,b)\neq 1
,
d=1
and
\min(a,b)<\frac{1}{2}
.

Let us temporarily use the notation
c=\min(a,b)
.
Similarly to (
49
), we have
\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2+c}=(3^{-u})^{-d/2+c}=(3^{d/2-c})^{u}
and
\frac{d}{2}-c>0
implies
3^{d/2-c}>1
so that

\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2+c}\leq\sum_{u=0}^{t}(3^{d/2-c})^{u}\leq\frac{(3^{d/2-c})^{t+1}}{3^{d/2-c}-1}\leq\frac{3^{d/2-c}}{3^{d/2-c}-1}\varepsilon_{t}^{-d/2+c}\leq M\varepsilon^{-d/2+c}\ \>.

We recall that we require
\varepsilon^{\alpha}\leq\delta
and writing
\delta=\varepsilon^{\beta}
, it amounts to require
0<\beta\leq\alpha<1
.
We can thus rewrites (
173
) as follows:

\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}\left(\widehat{\nu}_{\delta,N},\nu\right)\right]\\
\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}(\widehat{W}_{\delta,N},W_{S})\right]\end{array}\right\}\leq M\mu(D^{\delta})\left(\varepsilon^{(1-\alpha)\frac{d}{2}}+\varepsilon^{c}+\delta^{c}+\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\delta^{\frac{d}{2}}}\right)+M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{20C\delta})\>,

and we are left with carefully choosing
\alpha
and
\delta=\varepsilon^{\beta}
satisfying
0<\beta\leq\alpha<1
. Note that
\alpha
is a parameter of the proof while
\delta
is a parameter of the estimators
\widehat{\nu}_{\delta,N}
,
\widehat{W}_{\delta,N}
.
Let us first focus on the
3
terms involving
\delta
:
\displaystyle\delta^{c}=\varepsilon^{\beta c}
,
\displaystyle\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\delta^{\frac{d}{2}}}=\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{d}{2}\beta}
and
\displaystyle\delta^{d-l}=\varepsilon^{(d-l)\beta}
, since
(d-l)\geq 1\geq c
, we can optimize
\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\beta c}+\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}-\frac{d}{2}\beta}
which gives

\beta c=\frac{d}{2}-\frac{d}{2}\beta\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad\beta=\frac{d}{d+2c}

and since
\displaystyle\frac{dc}{d+2c}\leq c\leq 1
we then have

\displaystyle\max\left\{\delta^{c},\>\left(\frac{\varepsilon}{\delta}\right)^{\frac{d}{2}},\>\delta^{d-l},\varepsilon^{c}\right\}\leq\varepsilon^{\displaystyle\frac{dc}{d+2c}}
.
Furthermore minimizing
\displaystyle\varepsilon^{\frac{dc}{d+2c}}+\varepsilon^{(1-\alpha)\frac{d}{2}}
we obtain

(1-\alpha)\frac{d}{2}=\frac{dc}{d+2c}\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad\alpha=\frac{d}{d+2\min(a,b)}=\beta

so that we have
\varepsilon^{\alpha}\leq\delta
is satisfied (actually with equality
\delta=\varepsilon^{\alpha}
) and (
174
) is proved for
\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}<0
.

– Case
\min(a,b)=\frac{d}{2}
:
which corresponds to
d=2
and
a=b=1
or
d=1
and
\min(a,b)=\frac{1}{2}
.
In this case,
\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}=\varepsilon^{c}
,
\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2+c}=1
and then

\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2+c}\leq\varepsilon^{c}t\leq M\varepsilon^{c}|\ln\varepsilon|\>.

The
3
terms involving
\delta
in the right hand side of (
173
) are unchanged and the optimization again yields
\delta=\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{d+2c}}=\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}
(since
2c=d
) and we note that for
\varepsilon\in(0,1]
,
\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}|\ln\varepsilon|\leq 1
,
\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}|\ln\varepsilon|\leq 2
, and thus

\varepsilon^{c}|\ln\varepsilon|=\left\{\begin{array}[]{lcl}\varepsilon|\ln\varepsilon|\leq\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}=\delta=\delta^{c}&\text{if}&d=2,\>c=1\\
\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}|\ln\varepsilon|\leq 2\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{4}}=2\delta^{c}&\text{if}&d=1,\>c=\frac{1}{2}\end{array}\right\}\leq 2\delta^{c}=2\delta^{\min(a,b)}

and we obtain the same control (
174
) for
\min(a,b)=\frac{d}{2}
.

– Case
\min(a,b)-\frac{d}{2}>0
:

in this case
d=1
and we have
\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2+c}=\left(3^{\frac{1}{2}-c}\right)^{u}
, and
3^{\frac{1}{2}-c}<1
thus

\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}\sum_{u=s+1}^{t}\varepsilon_{u}^{-d/2+c}\leq M\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2}}|\ln\varepsilon|=M\varepsilon^{\frac{1}{2(1+2c)}}|\ln\varepsilon|\varepsilon^{\frac{c}{1+2c}}\leq M\varepsilon^{\frac{c}{1+2c}}=M\delta^{c}

so that the control by
\delta^{c}
still holds in this last case and we conclude Step
3
and the proof of (
174
).

Step
4
:
we now compare
\widehat{V}_{\delta,N}
and
\widehat{W}_{\delta,N}
and show that

(175)

\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{D}\left(\widehat{V}_{\delta,N},\widehat{W}_{\delta,N}\right)\right]\leq M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{20C\delta})+M\delta^{\min(a,b)}\>\mu(D)\quad\text{with}\quad\delta=\delta_{N}=N^{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{d+2\min(a,b)}}\>.

At this step, we let
f\in{\rm C}_{c}(\mathbb{R}^{n}\times{\rm Sym}_{+}(n))
be a
1
–Lipschitz function satisfying
\supp f\subset D\times{\rm Sym}_{+}(n)
as in case
(ii)
and such that
\|f\|_{\infty}\leq 1
. As in Step
1
, we write
D=(D\cap S\cap\mathfrak{S}^{10C\delta})\sqcup T
and then

\displaystyle\left|\int_{D\times{\rm Sym}_{+}(n)}\right.

\displaystyle\left.\hskip-1.0ptf\>d\widehat{V}_{\delta,N}-\int_{D\times{\rm Sym}_{+}(n)}\hskip-1.0ptf\>d\widehat{W}_{\delta,N}\right|=\int_{D}\left|f(x,\pi_{\delta,N}(x))-f(x,\sigma_{\delta,N}(x))\right|\Phi\left(\theta_{\delta,N}(x)\right)\>d\mu_{N}(x)

\displaystyle\leq\|\Phi\|_{\infty}\int_{T}\left\|\pi_{\delta,N}(x)-\sigma_{\delta,N}(x)\right\|\>d\mu_{N}(x)+2\|\Phi_{\infty}\|\|f\|_{\infty}\mu_{N}\left(\mathfrak{S}^{10C\delta}\cap D\cap S\right)

\displaystyle\leq M\left(\int_{T}\left\|\Pi_{T_{x}S}-\sigma_{\delta,N}(x)\right\|\>d\mu_{N}(x)+\mu_{N}\left(\mathfrak{S}^{10C\delta}\cap D\right)\right)\quad\text{thanks to }\eqref{eqStabSigmardeltaN}\>.

The right hand side above is independent of
f
and therefore, taking the supremum with respect to
f
and then the mean value, we obtain thanks to (
162
) and (
146
):

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\beta_{B}\left(\widehat{V}_{\delta,N},\widehat{W}_{\delta,N}\right)\right]

\displaystyle\leq M\left(\mathbb{E}_{\bf X}\left[\int_{D}\mathbb{E}_{\tilde{\bf Y},{\bf Z}}\left[\left\|\Pi_{T_{x}S}-\sigma_{\delta,N}^{\tilde{\bf Y},{\bf Z}}\right\|\right]\>d\mu_{N}^{\bf X}\right]+\mu\left(D\cap\mathfrak{S}^{10C\delta}\right)\right)

\displaystyle\leq M\mu(D)\left(\delta^{\min(a,b)}+\frac{\varepsilon^{\frac{d}{2}}}{\delta^{\frac{d}{2}}}\right)+M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{20C\delta})

which conclude the proof of (
175
) with
\displaystyle\delta=\delta_{N}=N^{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{d+2\min(a,b)}}
.

We conclude the proof of (
7.4
) by triangular inequality on the Bounded Lipschitz distance, combining (
174
) and (
175
).

Step
5
:
In the particular case where
D=B
is a ball of radius
R_{B}\leq\frac{R}{3}
and
N
is large enough so that
\delta_{N}\leq R_{B}
, we have the local estimate (
7.4
). Indeed, for
l\in\{0,1,\ldots,d-1\}
, if
B^{20C\delta}\cap\mathfrak{S}_{l}=\emptyset
then
\mathcal{H}^{l}(B^{20C\delta}\cap\mathfrak{S}_{l})=0
and otherwise, there exists
x\in\mathfrak{S}_{l}
such that
B\subset B(x,2R_{B}+20C\delta)\subset B(x,(2+20C)R_{B})
and
recalling (

H
4

) and (

H
6

), we have:
\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap B(x,(2+20C)R_{B}))\leq MR_{B}^{l}
so that by Ahlfors regularity of
\mu
,

\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap B^{20C\delta})\leq M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\xi_{l}\delta^{d-l}R_{B}^{l}\leq M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\xi_{l}\frac{\delta^{d-l}}{R_{B}^{d-l}}\mu(B)\>.

Since we also have
\mu(B^{\delta})\leq\mu(2B)\leq M\mu(B)
, we conclude the proof of (
7.4
) thanks to (
7.4
).
∎

We finally mention the simpler following
\|\cdot\|_{{\rm L}^{1}(\mu)}
convergences that hold under the same assumptions as those of Theorem
7.4
.

Proposition 7.5
.

Let
0<a,b\leq 1
.
We assume that
S
and
\theta
satisfy assumptions
(H_{1})
to (

H
7

) i.e.
\mu=\theta\mathcal{H}^{d}_{|S}\in\mathcal{P}
. Let
D\subset\mathbb{R}^{n}
be an open set.
Let
N\in\mathbb{N}^{\ast}
be large enough so that
\delta_{N}:=N^{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{d+2\min(a,b)}}\leq\frac{R}{2C}
. Then

\displaystyle\left.\begin{array}[]{c}\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{D}|\theta_{\delta_{N},N}-\theta|\>d\mu\right]\\
\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{D}\|\sigma_{\delta_{N},N}(x)-\Pi_{T_{x}S}\|\right]\>d\mu\end{array}\right\}\leq M\mu(D)\delta_{N}^{\min(a,b)}+M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta_{N}^{d-l}\mathcal{H}^{l}\left(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{4C\delta_{N}}\right)\leq\widetilde{M}\>\delta_{N}^{\min(a,b)}\>,

with
\widetilde{M}=M\left(1+\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\mathcal{H}^{l}\left(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\right)\right)
.

In the particular case where
\mathfrak{S}=\emptyset
, we also have the uniform estimates: for all
x\in S
,

(178)

\mathbb{E}\left[|\theta_{\delta_{N},N}(x)-\theta(x)|\right]\leq M\delta_{N}^{\min(a,b)}\quad\text{and}\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\|\sigma_{\delta_{N},N}(x)-\Pi_{T_{x}S}\|\right]\leq M\delta_{N}^{\min(a,b)}\>.

Proof.

We apply Proposition
3.5
and Proposition
6.12
so that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{D}|\theta_{\delta_{N},N}-\theta|\>d\mu\right]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{D}\mathbb{E}\left[|\theta_{\delta_{N},N}-\theta_{\delta_{N}}|\right]\>d\mu+\int_{D}|\theta_{\delta_{N}}-\theta|\>d\mu

\displaystyle\leq M\mu(D)\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\delta_{N}^{d}}}+\delta_{N}^{\min(a,b)}\right)+M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta_{N}^{d-l}\>\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{2C\delta_{N}})

and we infer (
7.5
) (first line) since we recall that with
\delta_{N}=N^{\displaystyle-\frac{1}{d+2\min(a,b)}}
, we have
\displaystyle\frac{1}{\sqrt{N\delta_{N}^{d}}}=\delta_{N}^{\min(a,b)}
.

Similarly applying Proposition
5.6
and Proposition
6.13
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{D}\|\sigma_{\delta_{N},N}(x)-\Pi_{T_{x}S}\|\>d\mu\right]

\displaystyle\leq\int_{D}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\sigma_{\delta_{N},N}-\sigma_{\delta_{N},\delta_{N}}\|\right]\>d\mu+\int_{D}\|\sigma_{\delta_{N},\delta_{N}}(x)-\Pi_{T_{x}S}\|\>d\mu

\displaystyle\leq M\mu(D)\left(\frac{2}{\sqrt{N\delta_{N}^{d}}}+2\delta_{N}^{\min(a,b)}\right)+M\sum_{l=0}^{d-1}\delta_{N}^{d-l}\>\mathcal{H}^{l}(\mathfrak{S}_{l}\cap D^{4C\delta_{N}})

and we conclude the proof of (
7.5
). The proof of (
178
) is a straightforward application of the aforementioned Propositions
3.5
,
6.12

5.6
and
6.13

∎
